Length 10
(3((3((3((3((3((3((3((3((3((3x+1)/2^k1)+1)/2^k2)+1)/2^k3)+1)/2^k4)+1)/2^k5)+1)/2^k6)+1)/2^k7)+1)/2^k8)+1)/2^k9)+1)/2^k10 = x
x = (3^9 + 3^8·2^k1 + 3^7·2^{k1+···+k2} + 3^6·2^{k1+···+k3} + 3^5·2^{k1+···+k4} + 3^4·2^{k1+···+k5} + 3^3·2^{k1+···+k6} + 3^2·2^{k1+···+k7} + 3·2^{k1+···+k8} + 2^{k1+···+k9}) / (2^{k1+···+k10} - 3^10)
The least odd member x > 1 has first exponent 1: any larger power of 2 sends (3x+1)/2^k below x.
Later, exponent k at step j satisfies k < j*(log2(3)-1)+2, checked as 2^{k+j-2} < 3^j.
Maximum k at steps 0..9: 1 2 3 3 4 4 5 6 6 7
The total K = k1+...+k10 must satisfy 2^K > 3^10, i.e. K >= ceil(10 log2(3)). A prefix that cannot reach that total, even at the maximum legal later exponents, is rejected once.
A rejected prefix rules out every continuation. SOLUTION would be an odd cycle with x > 1.
x<=C < L: staying at or above the minimum forces x <= C, below the lower bound L.
